कि कर प्रदाय नहीं है या डीटीएए के अनुसार विशेष दर पर प्रदाय है, धटाई गई है, सम्मिलित है, यदि कोई है ।

MINISTRY OF FINANCE 
(Department of Revenue) 
(CENTRAL BOARD OF DIRECT TAXES) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 158 March, 2024 
G.S.R 153(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by section 139 read with section 295 of the Income- 


tax Act, 1961 (43 of 1961), the Central Board of Direct Taxes hereby makes the following rules further to amend 
the Income-tax Rules, 1962, namely:- 


1. Short title and commencement.— (1) These rules may be called the Income-tax (Third Amendment) 
Rules, 2024.

(2) They shall into f he 1“ day of April, 2024
